- [Jul-01] **Arthur:** **SHIP @ 61c8363** — cold-boot ~3s retime. Verified LIVE in Chromium (fonts aborted; sampled computed styles across 50→3800ms both motion+reduce, 0 pageerrors). **The 3s reads as a deliberate premium power-on** (Mass Effect / Cyberpunk cadence — 3s is inside the 2–4s ceremony window) and **the ~2.3→3.0s hold is genuinely ALIVE, not a frozen card** — verified numerically: two independent oscillations run through the hold, wordmark brightness 1.0→1.22 (breath inhale) WHILE sys-line opacity .85→.5 (pulse dim). No dead gap sweep-end→hold (breath@1900=logoIn end, pulse@2350=sysIn end — seamless, no snap); glitch finishes 1460ms so it lands clean before the 1900ms resolve; the 1700ms/82vh sweep is a deliberate single scan (~48vh/s), not sluggish. Exit self-removes ~3.26–3.36s. **Two folds routed to @Kaito (NEITHER blocks the pacing):** (1) *[a11y, recommend before gate]* under `prefers-reduced-motion:reduce` the user gets a STATIC card held 3s (all motion killed by the wildcard) — the 3s only exists to show motion they opted out of → shorten their floor: `MIN = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches ? 900 : 3000` (~1 line, reuse proven 900ms, no new magic number). (2) *[fast-follow]* at 3s×every-reload the boot now taxes power-users who refresh → add sessionStorage session-skip: first cold-open=3000ms, repeat same-session loads=~900ms (NOT zeroed — flash risk); use sessionStorage so a genuine cold-start/PWA-relaunch earns the full ceremony again. **@Akashi** #2 touches storage (one boolean flag, ephemeral, no PII/network) → trivially safe but your nod before Kaito builds. - [Jul-01] **Arthur:** **INTERACTION SYSTEM — "every element senses touch & cursor" (Osefe's directive). Full tiered build-spec below + in `team/logs/arthur.md`.** AUDIT VERDICT: **the room breathes, but the furniture doesn't move when you touch it.** Ambient life is already rich (Desktop Alive / Lock Alive / cold-boot / cursor-wake / gridPulse) — but DIRECT-INPUT response is thin: only the 3 primary control classes (`.btn`, `.tab`, `.scen-btn`) got press+hover+focus (my earlier Tier-1). Everything you actually TAP below that tier is a PNG. That's exactly the "2016 feel" Osefe is reacting to. Fix = a reusable interaction LAYER, not one-offs.

  **DEAD / PNG (no press, no hover, no focus-ring, some no transition at all):**
  • `.item-row` (index.html:379) — list rows across expenses/subs/media/PRs/reminders. **The single biggest gap** — zero :active, zero hover, zero transition, no focus-visible. Rows are the most-tapped surface and they're stone.
  • `.sub-item` (297) — dead, no transition.
  • `.exp-head` (314) — has hover bg + transition, but NO press, NO focus-visible (it's a clickable div, not keyboard-reachable).
  • `.cal-cell` (392) / `.cal-nav button` (387) / `.catsw` (227) — hover only, no press, no focus-visible.
  • `.localebar .iconbtn` (752) / `.savebtn` (405) / `.savefab` (413) / `.chattoggle` (415) / `.lockcard .lk-btn` (445) — hover only, no :active press.
  • Inputs/selects/textareas (`.field` 361, lockcard 443, chat 424, localebar 551) — cyan focus glow exists, but no label-motion, no animated underline, no keyboard focus-visible ring.
  • `.modal`/`.modal-back` (648-651) — SNAP open/close, no enter/leave.
  • `toast()` (7982) — SNAPS in (only fades out).
  • `.check li .box` (281) / `.loantoggle` (346) — minimal, no press.
  • **Value-change motion — NONE.** hero-num/bignum/calc-out/stat vals/group totals SNAP to new values. Item A (count-up) still deferred on the "32 000 kr" parse problem — but a pulse-on-change needs no parsing and is the honest interim "living data" cue.
  • **No touch ripple anywhere. No cursor-reactive tilt/glare** (cards only translateY-lift on hover).

  **ALREADY ALIVE (BUILD ON, don't duplicate):** `.tab` hover(-2px)+active(.97)+focus-visible+tabMoved (203-220); `.btn`/`.scen-btn` hover+active+focus (369-373, 342-345); `.card` desktop hover-lift 2px/180ms (92-95); gridPulse/ambientBreath/hudGlowPulse/scanSlow; cursor parallax ±3.5px; cursor-wake gutters; exp-body max-height accordion + rowIn stagger (321-340); `.exp-head:hover`, `.cal-cell:hover`, native focus glow on inputs. The focus-visible rule (213) is the SEED — Tier 1 just widens its selector list.

  **SYSTEM — shared tokens (add to :root), reused by every primitive (no magic numbers):**
  `--press: transform .09s cubic-bezier(.2,0,0,1), filter .09s ease, box-shadow .12s ease;`
  `--ease-out: cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1);`  `--hover-t:.16s var(--ease-out);`
  `--focus-ring: 0 0 0 4px rgba(124,255,178,.14);`
  Press magnitude scales INVERSELY with element size (premium, not rubber-toy): small btn/chip `scale(.95)`; medium btn `scale(.97)`; **large surfaces (rows/cards/cells) `scale(.99) + filter:brightness(1.06)`** (a "push-in," not a shrink). All GPU (transform/opacity/filter only).

  ==== **TIER 1 — GLOBAL PRESS + HOVER + FOCUS. The PNG-killer. Build this first — highest impact-per-effort, one CSS block (~70-100 lines), pure presentation, no JS, no i18n, no Akashi.** ====
  **1a — Universal :active press** (fires on touch AND mouse; `<100ms`). Add `transition:var(--press)` + `:active` to every dead/partial interactive element:
   - Rows/large: `.item-row, .sub-item, .exp-head, .cal-cell { transition:var(--press); } .item-row:active,.sub-item:active,.exp-head:active,.cal-cell:active{ transform:scale(.99); filter:brightness(1.06); }`
   - Buttons missing it: `.localebar .iconbtn, .cal-nav button, .catsw, .savebtn, .savefab, .chattoggle, .lockcard .lk-btn, .chatx, .modal-actions button, .ib-x { transition:var(--press); } …:active{ transform:scale(.96); filter:brightness(1.05); }` (ib-go already has translateY(1px) — leave or unify to scale(.96)).
   - Checkbox/toggle: `.check li .box:active{transform:scale(.9)} .loantoggle:active{transform:scale(.99);filter:brightness(1.06)}`
   NOTE: rows scroll — keep row press SUBTLE (.99 + brightness, no translate) so a scroll-drag never reads as a bounce; `touch-action:manipulation` is already global so no 300ms tap delay.
  **1b — Universal hover, DESKTOP-GATED** `@media (hover:hover) and (pointer:fine){…}` (never sticks on touch, zero mobile cost). Tuned per element, not blanket:
   - Rows/cells (a lift on a full-width row looks wrong → brighten instead): `.item-row:hover,.sub-item:hover{border-color:var(--cyan-dim);background:rgba(0,229,255,.045)}` `.cal-cell:hover` already good.
   - Small buttons: `.localebar .iconbtn:hover,.cal-nav button:hover,.catsw:hover,.savebtn:hover,.chattoggle:hover` → border-brighten + `transform:translateY(-2px)` (reuse the 2px depth vocabulary; several already lift — just add the missing ones).
  **1c — Focus-visible EVERYWHERE (a11y + keyboard, currently missing on ~everything but .btn/.tab/.scen-btn).** Widen rule 213 to a full focusable list:
   `a, button, input, select, textarea, [tabindex], .item-row, .exp-head, .cal-cell, .iconbtn, .catsw, .check li .box { }` → `…:focus-visible{ outline:2px solid var(--lime); outline-offset:2px; box-shadow:var(--focus-ring); }`. Inputs KEEP their cyan glow (365) AND get the lime keyboard ring (belt-and-braces, high visibility). Clickable DIVs that need keyboard reach (`.exp-head`, `.cal-cell`, actionable `.item-row`) need `tabindex="0"` + `role="button"` — scope carefully: DON'T make rows focusable if their only actions are buttons already inside them (those focus already); DO make `.exp-head` (the accordion toggle) and `.cal-cell` keyboard-operable.
  **EFFORT:** ~1 session, one consolidated CSS block. **RISK:** (a) sticky-hover on touch → the `(hover:hover)` gate kills it; (b) press-scale twitch during list scroll → keep rows at .99+brightness, no translate; (c) focus-visible on divs needs tabindex — scope to genuinely-actionable divs only, and verify Enter/Space fire the same handler. **i18n: none. Akashi: none (pure CSS/presentation).** This tier ALONE kills ~90% of the PNG feel.

  ==== **TIER 2 — INPUT AFFORDANCES + VALUE-CHANGE PULSE + MODAL/TOAST TRANSITIONS + TOUCH RIPPLE.** (medium; CSS + tiny vanilla JS) ====
  **2a — Input life:** on `.field:focus-within label{color:var(--cyan);transform:translateY(-1px)}` (transition .16s) + an animated underline via `.field { position relative } .field input+`—actually cleanest: a `::after` under the input that `transform:scaleX(0)→scaleX(1)` from center on `:focus`, 180ms var(--ease-out), cyan. Reduced-motion → instant color only. Apply to `.field`, `.lockcard`, `.chatbody`, `.localebar`.
  **2b — Value-change PULSE (the "living data" win, NO number-parsing → safe now, unlike deferred item A):** keyframe `@keyframes valFlash{0%{transform:scale(1)}40%{transform:scale(1.06);filter:brightness(1.25)}100%{transform:scale(1);filter:none}}` 320ms var(--ease-out). Helper `pulseValue(el)` adds `.val-flash` (removes on animationend). CALL it in the existing render/refresh paths (refreshFinance, setHeroLeftover, calc renderers, group-total render) — **only when the string actually changed** (compare `el.dataset.prev` to new textContent; skip if equal, or it strobes on every unrelated re-render — same care as item E's re-trigger bug, but string-compare not parse). Optional color: up=lime, down=amber (compare numeric sign if easy; else neutral brightness). Applies: `.hero-num`, `.bignum`, `.calc-out`, `header.hud .stat .val`, `.exp-head .gtot`, streak. **RISK: must gate on real change (prev≠next) or it flashes constantly. Akashi: wraps the poison-gated number setters but reads/adds no figure → flag for a glance, it's presentation.**
  **2c — Modal enter/leave:** `.modal-back{transition:opacity .18s} .modal{transform:scale(.96) translateY(6px);opacity:0;transition:transform .18s var(--ease-out),opacity .18s}` `.modal-back.show .modal{transform:none;opacity:1}`; close reverses ~140ms. **2d — Toast enter:** current `toast()` (7982) injects with no entrance → add `transform:translateY(-12px) scale(.98);opacity:0` then rAF→`translateY(0) scale(1);opacity:1`, 220ms var(--ease-out) (keep the existing fade-out). **2e — Touch ripple (touch-first):** delegated `pointerdown` listener spawns a `span.ripple` at press-origin inside a broad selector (`.btn,.tab,.item-row,.exp-head,[data-ripple]`); CSS radial cyan low-alpha `scale(0)→scale(2.2)` + fade 450ms var(--ease-out), element needs `position:relative;overflow:hidden` (rounded rows already clip fine). Reduced-motion → skip spawn. **EFFORT: 1-2 sessions. RISK: 2b change-gating; ripple overflow/perf; keep will-change OFF static (only during ripple). i18n: none. Akashi: 2b glance only.**

  ==== **TIER 3 — CURSOR-REACTIVE (desktop) + RICHER LIST ADD/REMOVE.** (richest, highest taste/perf risk — desktop-gated) ====
  **3a — Card glare toward cursor:** `pointermove` on hovered `.card` (gated `(hover:hover)+(pointer:fine)+min-width:1024px`+no-preference) sets `--mx/--my`; a `.card::after` radial highlight positioned at cursor (opacity ~.06 cyan). rAF, `will-change:transform` toggled ON only while hovered, OFF on leave (will-change budget). **Optional micro-tilt ≤3deg** rotateX/rotateY — CAP hard or numbers get hard to read; I lean **glare-only, no tilt** for a serious finance tool (tilt is the toy-risk). **3b — Button pointer-sheen:** `--mx`-driven linear-gradient highlight sweeping under the cursor on `.btn`/`.tab` (desktop). **3c — List add/remove enter-leave** (this is deferred item C, now buildable): a `rowBorn` lime flash on inserted rows + a collapse-fade on removal — needs stable per-row identity threaded through the render (the render-identity work Akashi & I flagged on item E/C). **EFFORT: 1-2 sessions. RISK: tilt/glare perf on low-end (desktop gate mitigates), will-change budget (per-hover toggle, never static on N cards), taste (cap glare low, skip tilt). 3c needs render-identity. i18n: none. Akashi: 3c touches render path.**

  **BUILD ORDER:** Tier 1 (one CSS block, immediate PNG-kill) → 2b value-pulse + 2c/2d modal/toast (fast wins) → 2a inputs + 2e ripple → Tier 3. Tier 1 is buildable RIGHT NOW with zero dependencies.

  **HARD CONSTRAINTS (baked into every primitive):** transform/opacity/filter ONLY (no layout thrash, 60fps mid-phone); every motion under `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference)` with a still fallback (the global reduce-wildcard 513-514 also catches leaks); real `:focus-visible` states; tap targets ≥44px (medType/Filter/prSeg already 44px; verify iconbtn 44 via `#openSaveBtn` precedent); pure CSS + tiny vanilla JS, NO libs; touch gets press/ripple, desktop gets hover/cursor-reactive — neither pays the other's cost; will-change only during active interaction; **does NOT regress** Desktop Alive / Lock Alive / boot / cursor-wake (all use different elements/properties — verified no collision).

  **LOADING DOTS (#boot .boot-dots, f2c7302) — BLESSED, ON-SYSTEM. VERDICT: SHIP.** Reviewed the code (index.html:124-133): 3×5px cyan dots, gap 8px, glow, a traveling opacity+`translateY(-2px)` pulse wave (bootDot 1.4s ease-in-out, staggered .24s/.48s), fades in at 1850ms (bootDotsIn), `aria-hidden`, base `opacity:0` so under reduced-motion (bootDotsIn only runs no-preference) they stay invisible — correctly hidden under reduce. This matches the system voice: GPU (opacity+transform), subtle, motion-gated, premium restraint. Timing is coherent — they fade in at 1850ms exactly as the 1900→3000ms living-hold (wordmark-breath + sys-pulse) begins, so they read as "system standing by," NOT a fake-wait spinner (the boot still exits on max(ready,3000), so nothing implies real waiting). ONE optional restraint note (NOT a change request): the `translateY(-2px)` bounce is the only faintly-playful beat — at 2px it's within the app's 2px-lift vocabulary so it's fine; if Osefe ever wants max-serious, drop to opacity-only (a pure "scanning" pulse). Also the code IS a traveling-glow wave across 3 always-present dots, which is cleaner than literal 1→2→3 add/remove — the line-122 comment slightly oversells it, no code change needed. Ship as built. — Arthur

## [2026-07-02] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — BUILD-SPEC: THE FUTURISM WAVE (Tiers 1-3a shipped → "jaw-dropping"; Wave A/B/C)
- Asked: Osefe wants MAX impressiveness — "as FUTURISTIC as possible, tons of animations," BOTH desktop+mobile, feel like the most advanced thing he's touched. Design the definitive tiered build-spec taking MRLN from responsive (Tiers 1-3a, v19/v20) to jaw-dropping. Director-only, Kaito builds. HARD RAILS: locked 60fps mid-phone (transform/opacity/filter only, cap concurrent, will-change discipline, no perpetual rAF), ALL motion prefers-reduced-motion gated w/ dignified static fallback, serious register (no confetti/toy), FREE·OFFLINE (no libs/assets/network), don't regress shipped ambient/interaction layers, flag Akashi + i18n. MUST-INCLUDE queued: tap ripple 2e, button glint 3b, list add/remove 3c (solve render-identity concretely), input-focus :not(:focus-visible) polish, Tier-1.5 keyboard reach (F3).
- Studied / referenced: byte-grounded in the CURRENT file @ tip 9d2ec15 (Tier2+3a live, sw v20). Confirmed: tab handler `prevTabIdx`+`from-left/from-right` @2539-2546 → panels ALREADY slide (`slideFromR/L .22s cubic-bezier(.25,0,0,1)` @272-275, my item B); `fmtN` @2127 = `Math.round(n).toLocaleString(curInfo().loc)` — INTEGER-rounded (no decimals) so stripping every non-[0-9-] char = locale-agnostic parse (the key that makes parse-then-count SAFE); `#ovLeft` @1190 holds a PURE number, currency is a SIBLING `.ccySuffix` span → count-up-friendly targets; `expMonth`/`gtot` (@2723/2718) embed '~'/' kr'/'/mo' INLINE → NOT clean count-up targets; every list = wholesale `host.innerHTML = arr.map().join('')` (renderExpenses @2698-2721, savings @2996, media/PR/workouts) with NO data-id on rows yet; `.meter>span` @302-306 = gradient bar (span width); NO SVG progress ring shipped (only bodySvg @1579); `navigator.vibrate` = ZERO hits (haptics net-new); 2b liveVal MutationObserver already watches `.hero-num/.bignum/.calc-out/.stat .val` text (count-up MUST guard it or strobe); chat = `.chatwidget` display:none→block snap @2017/2044, `.chatbody` textarea. Refs (gluttony, named per item below): Cyberpunk 2077 boot/scan HUD, Destiny 2 orbit-load + reticle feedback, Iron Man JARVIS holo-assemble, Apple visionOS depth/materialize, Stripe/Linear entrance discipline, iOS UIButton haptic tick, Material state-layer ripple, Robinhood/Revolut number count-up, Android overscroll glow.
- Found / verdict: **BUILD-SPEC — decisive, 3 waves. Wave A = the jaw-drop per effort (numbers spin up, panels assemble like a HUD, every tap ripples + ticks) at locked 60fps; B/C at direction level. This is density-of-RESPONSIVENESS, not noise — every effect is short, GPU-only, gated, and dies to zero cost. Nothing loops forever; nothing fakes a wait.**

### SHARED TOKENS (add to :root once; every primitive reuses — no magic numbers)
- `--ease-out: cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)` (expo-out "settle into focus" — reuse if already present from Tier-1; the shipped valPulse uses this literal). `--ease-hud: cubic-bezier(.25,0,0,1)` (the existing panel-slide curve). `--count-dur:420ms; --ripple-dur:460ms; --draw-dur:700ms; --assemble-step:40ms`.

## WAVE A — SHIP FIRST (biggest visible wow ÷ effort; all touch+desktop; all under @media(prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference) + the global reduce wildcard backstop; all transform/opacity/filter)

**A1 — TAP RIPPLE (queued 2e). Ref: Material state-layer + iOS touch highlight. THE #1 "alive on touch" signal.**
- Mechanism: ONE delegated `pointerdown` listener on document. On a press whose target matches `.btn,.tab,.scen-btn,.item-row,.exp-head,.cal-cell,.chattoggle,.medSeedChip,[data-ripple]`, spawn `<span class="ripple">` positioned at `(e.clientX-rect.left, e.clientY-rect.top)` inside that element. Element gets `position:relative;overflow:hidden` (rounded rows/cards already clip). Keyframe `ripple{from{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(0);opacity:.5}to{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(2.4);opacity:0}}`, `--ripple-dur` var(--ease-out), radial `rgba(0,229,255,.35)`. Remove the span on `animationend`.
- Curve/timing: 460ms var(--ease-out). Perf: one short-lived span/press, `will-change` NOT set (too brief to matter), removed on end → zero residue. Where: every primary/secondary tappable + the big list rows. Risk: overflow clipping on non-clipped ancestors (scope selector to elements already border-radius'd / add overflow:hidden to the ripple set only); double-fire with 2b — none (different elements/props). Reduced-motion: listener early-returns (no spawn). i18n: none. Akashi: none (no data/state read; pure DOM decoration).

**A2 — HAPTIC TICK (navigator.vibrate). Ref: iOS UIButton haptic — mobile's answer to hover.**
- Mechanism: in the SAME A1 pointerdown path, `if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(8)` — a single 8ms tick — but ONLY for PRIMARY controls (`.btn,.tab,.scen-btn,.savebtn,.chattoggle`), NOT rows/cells (buzzing on every scroll-tap = noise). pointerdown IS the required user-gesture. Android-mostly (iOS Safari lacks the Vibration API → silently no-ops, correct graceful degrade). GATE it OFF under reduced-motion too (haptic is motion-adjacent; sensory-sensitive users opt out via the same switch).
- Perf: 8ms tick, negligible battery. Risk: over-buzz → scoped to primaries only + 8ms (a tick, not a rumble). i18n: none. **Akashi: FLAG for the nod — navigator.vibrate is a benign device-capability call, no data/network/storage, but it's a new platform API so route it (expect trivially SAFE).**

**A3 — NUMBER COUNT-UP (the signature futurism move; = deferred item A, NOW solvable). Ref: Revolut/Robinhood balance count-up + Cyberpunk HUD spin-up. Composes with the shipped 2b pulse.**
- THE PARSE PROBLEM, SOLVED CONCRETELY: fmtN rounds to INTEGER → no decimals → stripping every char except `[0-9-]` removes ALL locale grouping separators (da-DK '.', en-US ',', the NBSP space) safely, yielding the pure integer, in EVERY locale. And the clean targets (`#ovLeft`, `.hero-num`, `.stat .val`, `.calc-out`) hold a number-ONLY string (currency lives in a sibling `.ccySuffix` span) → nothing else to parse. This is why parse-then-count is safe now where my original parseFloat spec wasn't.
- Mechanism: helper `countUp(el, toValue)` — (1) read start = current on-screen value via `parseInt(el.textContent.replace(/[^0-9-]/g,''),10)`; NaN/'—' → start 0 (first-paint counts 0→N = the dashboard-boot flourish, pairs with the cold-boot). (2) if `Math.abs(to-start) / Math.max(Math.abs(to),1) < 0.02` OR start===to → `el.textContent=fmtN(to)` instantly, return (2% floor kills strobe on trivial slider deltas). (3) cancel any in-flight `el._cuRAF`; rAF-tween start→to over `--count-dur` (420ms) var(--ease-out); each frame `el.textContent = fmtN(cur)` (grouping stays locale-correct every frame); on end set exactly `fmtN(to)` + store `el._cuVal=to`.
- INTEGRATION (call-site, not observer — cleaner): replace the direct `el.textContent = fmtN(x)` at the clean sinks — setHeroLeftover, the #ovLeft/#ovComfort bignum writes, stat-val writes, .calc-out — with `countUp(el, x)`. countUp lands EXACTLY on `fmtN(x)` (the same poison-gated value the sink already computed) → it animates the DISPLAY only, adds/reads no figure. Slider-drag case handled elegantly: start = current on-screen value so rapid drags CHASE smoothly, and the 2% floor snaps tiny deltas → responsive, never laggy.
- **2b COMPOSE/GUARD (must-do, or it strobes):** the shipped liveVal observer watches text changes → countUp's per-frame writes would re-fire it every frame. FIX: countUp sets `el._cuAnimating=true` at start / clears on end; the liveVal observer early-returns while `el._cuAnimating` (so valPulse fires ONCE — the "reacted" punch — when countUp STARTS, then count-up does the settle; pulse+count-up compose exactly as I flagged in the Tier-2 review). Route the 1-line observer guard to Kaito.
- Curve/timing: 420ms var(--ease-out). Perf: rAF ONLY during the tween, cancels on complete — NO perpetual loop (battery rail honored). Where Wave A: `.hero-num`, #ovLeft/#ovComfort, `header.hud .stat .val`, `.calc-out` (all number-only). DEFER (→ Wave C): expMonth/gtot (inline '~'/kr/'/mo' — need a digit-span wrap; 2b pulse covers them meanwhile). Risk: re-entrancy w/ 2b (the guard) + landing exactly on fmtN(to) (spec'd). Reduced-motion: set final instantly (no tween). i18n: none. **Akashi: glance only — countUp re-formats the SAME value the sink computed, no new figure, same class as the 2b pass he already SAFE'd.**

**A4 — METER DRAW-ON. Ref: Destiny 2 / CP2077 bars that fill on reveal.**
- Mechanism: `.meter>span` currently sets width inline. Switch the fill to GPU: `transform-origin:left; transform:scaleX(var(--p))` where `--p` = 0..1 set inline per render; `transition:transform var(--draw-dur) var(--ease-out)`. On (re)render the bar animates from its prior scale to the new one; on first paint from 0. (scaleX stretches the 90deg gradient imperceptibly on a 14px bar — acceptable; avoids the layout cost of animating width.)
- Curve/timing: 700ms var(--ease-out). Perf: pure transform, GPU, one-shot per render. Where: every `.meter` (savings progress, budget bars). Risk: gradient stretch (negligible at bar height). Reduced-motion: wildcard kills the transition → bar just appears at target. i18n: none. Akashi: none.

**A5 — PANEL-ASSEMBLE on tab switch (upgrade the current slide → HUD boot). Ref: JARVIS holo-assemble + visionOS materialize.**
- Osefe wants MORE than the current whole-panel slide. Keep the directional slide on the panel CONTAINER, ADD a staggered materialize of the panel's direct `> .card` children so the panel ASSEMBLES card-by-card like a HUD powering on. Keyframe `assembleIn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}` 260ms var(--ease-out); nth-child delay `calc(var(--assemble-step) * n)` (40ms step), CAP at 8 children (9th+ = 0 delay, snap) so a dense panel never feels slow. Total assemble < ~320ms.
- Where: `.panel.show > .card` (fires on every tab activation, the most-repeated moment → highest cumulative wow). Perf: only VISIBLE cards animate (~3-8), GPU transform/opacity, one-shot. Does NOT collide with the accordion rowIn (targets `.exp-body tr`, different element) or the slide (container vs children). Risk: repetitiveness across 17 tabs → keep short (260ms) + capped stagger; if Osefe finds it much, drop step to 30ms. Reduced-motion: wildcard kills it → cards just present (slide also gone → instant panel, dignified). i18n: none. Akashi: none.

**A6 — INPUT-FOCUS CONSISTENCY (the queued 1-liner from my Tier-2 review). a11y + system coherence.**
- 2a shipped `input:focus{outline:none}` which eats the Tier-1 unified lime keyboard `:focus-visible` outline on inputs. Scope the reset: `input:focus:not(:focus-visible),textarea:focus:not(:focus-visible),select:focus:not(:focus-visible){outline:none}` → mouse-focus keeps the clean cyan ring, keyboard-focus keeps the lime outline+ring like every other control. Pure CSS, no string. Fold into Wave A.

## WAVE B — RICH (medium effort; ship after A)
**B1 — LIST ADD/REMOVE ENTER-LEAVE (queued 3c; = deferred item C). SOLVES THE RENDER-IDENTITY PROBLEM without a VDOM/reconciler.** The app rebuilds `host.innerHTML` wholesale every render, so a removed node is destroyed before it can animate and an added node is indistinguishable. Concrete solution reusing THIS codebase's proven `just-opened` event-gate pattern (the item-E fix):
  - Give every list row a stable `data-id="<STATE item .id>"` in the map() template (media/PR/savings/workouts already carry `.id`; expenses render from MODEL.groups — key rows on `group+index` or add ids).
  - ENTER: module-level `justAdded` Set. Each add handler (applyChange add, MEDIALOG.add, addPR, addSavingsBox…) pushes the new id. After each render's `innerHTML=`, query `[data-id]` rows whose id ∈ justAdded → add `.row-enter` (`rowBorn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px);background:rgba(124,255,178,.14)}to{opacity:1;transform:none;background:transparent}}` 520ms var(--ease-out)), then CLEAR the set. Consuming the set each render = no re-trigger on unrelated re-renders (the exact discipline that fixed item E).
  - LEAVE: shared `animateRemove(rowEl, done)` — add `.row-leave` (`rowDie{to{opacity:0;transform:translateX(12px);max-height:0;padding:0;margin:0}}` 260ms ease-in), on animationend run `done()` = the REAL STATE mutation + re-render. Delete handlers call `animateRemove(el, ()=>{ …existing delete… })` instead of deleting immediately → animate-out THEN rebuild. Small local change per delete handler, no engine.
  - Perf: transform/opacity (+ max-height on leave — one row, acceptable). Reduced-motion: skip both classes → instant add/remove. **Akashi: FLAG — add/delete handlers may carry the figure poison; we only add a class + DEFER the existing mutation (don't touch figures) → glance on the deferred-delete path.** i18n: none.
**B2 — SAVINGS PROGRESS RING draw-on (= roadmap item J, net-new). Ref: Apple Activity ring / Destiny bounty ring.** ~44px SVG in the header pillbar beside 🔥 streak. Track `var(--line)` 3px; fill lime(on-pace)/amber(behind); `stroke-dasharray = C`, animate `stroke-dashoffset` from C→C*(1-progress) over 700ms var(--ease-out) on render. The "watch it grow" daily-return mechanic. Perf: SVG stroke, one-shot. **Akashi: pure local math, no network — flag per protocol (trivial).** i18n: none (numeric).
**B3 — BUTTON POINTER-GLINT (queued 3b). Ref: Stripe/Linear hover sheen; composes w/ shipped 3a card-sheen.** Desktop-gated `(hover:hover)+(pointer:fine)+min-width:1024px+no-preference`. `--mx` set on pointermove over `.btn/.tab`; a `::after` linear-gradient highlight tracks the cursor (rgba(255,255,255,.06)). rAF-coalesced (one write/frame), `will-change:transform` ON only while hovered / OFF on leave. Perf: desktop-only, tiny. Risk: will-change budget (per-hover toggle). i18n/Akashi: none.
**B4 — SCROLL-REVEAL (IntersectionObserver materialize). Ref: visionOS/modern editorial reveal.** Cards/sections `.card` fade+rise (translateY(16px)→0, opacity, 400ms var(--ease-out)) as they enter viewport, ONCE (unobserve after reveal — never re-animate on scroll-up). CRITICAL fail-safe: content starts VISIBLE; IO only ADDS a `.revealed` enhancement (if JS/IO absent OR reduced-motion → everything visible immediately, never trapped hidden). **Akashi: IntersectionObserver reads layout only, no data/net → flag for the nod (trivial).** i18n: none.
**B5 — OVERSCROLL GLOW (mobile). Ref: Android overscroll edge-glow.** A cyan glow band at the scroll boundary on over-pull (touch-driven, visual only). **I REJECT literal pull-to-refresh — honest cut: MRLN is a local offline app; there is no meaningful "refresh" action to pull for (nothing to re-fetch), so a pull-to-reload gesture would confuse (a 9-yo pulls, expects something, nothing happens or the app re-renders pointlessly).** The GLOW gives the tactile "edge of the data" feel without a phantom action. Mobile-only. Reduced-motion: skip. i18n/Akashi: none.
**B6 — ASSISTANT / CHAT SURFACE. Ref: JARVIS reveal + honest-latency discipline.** (a) Widget entrance: replace the display:none→block SNAP with scale(.96)+translateY(8px)+opacity→none, 200ms var(--ease-out) (mirror the shipped modal 2c). (b) Answer materialize: the answer computes instantly (local) — sell "intelligence" with a fast scan-line reveal of the answer text (a 240ms cyan wipe), NOT a fake typing delay. **HONEST CUT: no artificial "thinking…" wait — faking latency on an instant local answer is the exact anti-premium fake-wait I've argued against (Stripe/Linear discipline). Materialize fast, don't stall.** i18n: none (no new strings; motion only). Akashi: none.
**B7 — TIER-1.5 KEYBOARD REACH (queued F3; committed a11y, WCAG 2.1.1-A).** `.exp-head` + `.cal-cell` are click-only divs → not keyboard-operable (their focus-visible rings are currently inert). Add `tabindex="0"` + `role="button"` (+ `aria-expanded` on `.exp-head`) + a keydown handler firing the SAME click handler on Enter/Space (preventDefault on Space to stop scroll; guard against double-fire). Not "wow" but OWED — ships with the wave, not forgotten. i18n: none. Akashi: none (a11y wiring).

## WAVE C — DEEPEST (taste/perf risk; mostly desktop-gated; ship last)
- **C1 Header/HUD parallax depth + holographic accents** — extend the shipped Desktop-Alive cursor-parallax to layer the header (grid behind / hero mid / chrome front) with small differential offset on cursor (desktop) + subtle holographic hue-shift accent on the hero number band. Higher perf/taste risk → desktop-gated, capped offsets. Ref: visionOS depth, CP2077 layered HUD.
- **C2 Odometer per-digit roll** — upgrade A3 from a value-tween to a per-digit vertical roll (each digit a column that rolls to its target). Richer, but needs digit decomposition + handles the width reflow → effort/risk. Simple count-up (A3) is ~90% of the wow; this is the flourish. Ref: mechanical odometer / Robinhood.
- **C3 Count-up on inline-suffix numbers** — the deferred A3 half (expMonth/gtot): wrap just the digits in a `<span class="odo">` so count-up targets the digit span, leaving '~'/kr/'/mo' static. Finishes the count-up coverage.
- **C4 Glass/holographic card treatment + ambient data-glyphs** — subtle frosted-depth on cards + 2-3% opacity drifting data glyphs (extends the shipped ambient layer). Pure flourish; taste-gated.

## HONEST CUTS (things I'm NOT doing, and why — per the "cut only if it can't be done well" rule)
- **Literal pull-to-refresh** — no meaningful refresh in a local offline app; the gesture promises an action that doesn't exist → confusion. Replaced by overscroll GLOW (B5), which delivers the tactile feel without the phantom action.
- **Micro-tilt on cards** — toy-risk for a serious finance tool + hurts number legibility; glare/sheen (3a shipped, 3b B3) gives the cursor-reactive depth without the wobble. Consistent with my prior 3a call.
- **Fake "thinking…" delay on the assistant** — dishonest latency = anti-premium; B6 materializes fast instead.
- **Count-up on inline-suffix numbers in Wave A** — parse ambiguity with embedded '~'/kr/'/mo'; deferred to C3 via digit-span wrap; 2b pulse covers them meanwhile. Honest defer, not a skip.

## HARD-RAILS COMPLIANCE (baked into every item)
- 60fps mid-phone: transform/opacity/filter ONLY; no width/top/left layout animation (meter uses scaleX not width; ring uses stroke-dashoffset which is cheap on a tiny path). Concurrent-cap: ripple one-per-press, count-up cancels prior rAF per el, assemble capped at 8 children. will-change ONLY during active interaction (3b/3a), never static on N elements.
- No perpetual rAF: count-up/ripple/assemble/draw-on are all ONE-SHOT (rAF cancels on completion); the only continuous loops remain the existing idle-stopping ambient ones (gridPulse/Desktop-Alive/cursor-wake) — UNTOUCHED. Battery rail held.
- prefers-reduced-motion: every effect declared inside @media(no-preference) + the global `*{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}` reduce wildcard as backstop; JS listeners (ripple/haptic/countUp/scroll-reveal/glint) all early-return under reduce → zero cost + dignified static end-state (numbers land instantly, panels present, bars at target).
- Serious register: cyan/lime tokens only, no confetti/cartoon/bounce-overshoot; count-up settles (expo-out, no rubber), ripple is a clean scan not a splash.
- FREE·OFFLINE: zero libs, zero assets, zero network — vanilla JS + CSS + one small SVG (B2). System/bundled Orbitron+Share Tech Mono only.
- No-regress: A1 ripple / A5 assemble / B1 rows target DIFFERENT elements+props than Desktop-Alive/Lock-Alive/boot/cursor-wake/2b/3a (verified); the ONE coordination point is A3 count-up ↔ 2b observer (the `_cuAnimating` guard) — spec'd.
- i18n: NONE across the whole wave (motion/decoration only) → Mikoto no new keys.
- Akashi flags: A2 haptic (navigator.vibrate nod), B1 3c (deferred-delete path glance), B2 ring + B4 IO (local-math/layout-read nods) — all expected trivially SAFE; A3 count-up = glance-only (re-formats the same computed value, no new figure).

## BUILD ORDER (Wave A first, biggest wow ÷ effort)
A1 ripple + A2 haptic (one pointerdown path) → A3 count-up (+2b guard) → A4 meter draw-on + A6 input-focus 1-liner → A5 panel-assemble. Then Wave B (B1 lists → B2 ring → B7 a11y → B3/B4/B5/B6). Then Wave C on taste.

  **B4 ↔ A5 RESOLUTION — COEXIST by scope, never double-animate.** A5 (`.panel.show>.card` assembleIn, index.html:801-811) is SHIPPED + loved — do NOT replace it (rejecting B4-replaces-A5: throws away the on-every-tab-switch HUD-boot for a scroll effect most short panels never trigger). Rule: **A5 owns the cards visible on tab-switch; B4 takes over only the BELOW-FOLD cards, revealing them on scroll.** A card is EITHER A5 or B4, never both, because the B4 arm cancels A5 on that card (`animation:none`). CRITICAL fail-visible: the hidden state (`opacity:0`) is applied ONLY by JS, ONLY when IntersectionObserver + no-reduced-motion are both confirmed — so no-JS / no-IO / reduced-motion → every card visible, never trapped hidden.
  - CSS (add by A5, inside `@media(prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference)` so reduce can't hide anything): `.card.reveal-armed{opacity:0;transform:translateY(16px);animation:none!important}` and `.card.revealed{opacity:1;transform:none;transition:opacity 400ms var(--ease-out),transform 400ms var(--ease-out)}`.
  - JS: one module `IntersectionObserver` (`rootMargin:'0px 0px -8% 0px',threshold:.05`) that on-intersect does `remove('reveal-armed');add('revealed');unobserve(target)`. `armReveal(panel)`: early-return if no IO or reduced-motion; for each `panel.querySelectorAll(':scope>.card')` measure `getBoundingClientRect().top` — if `> innerHeight*0.85` → `add('reveal-armed')`+observe (hand to B4, cancels A5); else `remove('reveal-armed')` (leave to A5). Reset `remove('revealed')` at the top of each arm so a re-visit re-arms cleanly.
  - HOOK: in the tab handler (index.html:2600-2718), after the new panel gets `.show`, `requestAnimationFrame(function(){ armReveal(panelEl); })` (rAF so layout is settled → valid positions). Also call once on boot for the initial panel. Where B4 actually pays off: multi-card panels that exceed the fold (Overview, Connect, Stats, Savings). Single-tall-card list panels have no below-fold CARD to reveal (their rows scroll inside one card) — B4 correctly no-ops there. **Akashi: IO reads layout only, no data/net — trivial nod.**

  **B1 LIST ADD/REMOVE — build-ready. Every list item ALREADY carries a real `.id` from `uid()` → keying is trivial (no group+index hack needed, even expenses items have `it.id`).** SIX lists + their exact touchpoints:
  1. Expenses (Manage): `renderManage()` @3638 (rows already emit `data-del="it.id"`) · add: `addItem()` @~3559 · delete: `removeItem(id)` @3573 via delegated handler @3621 (inside `uiConfirm`).
  2. Media: `MEDIALOG.renderLists()` @7364 · add: `add()`@7289/`addSeed()`@7295 (unshift) · delete: `del(id)`@7298 via `data-delmed` @7421 (inside `uiConfirm`).
  3. PR: `renderPRs()` @4413 · add: prAdd @4104/4113 (push) · delete: `removePR(id)`@4125 via `data-del`@4121 (inside `uiConfirm`).
  4. Savings: `renderSavingsBoxes()` @3080 (row = the styled `<div>` @3105) · add: `sbSave()` new-branch @3163 · delete: `sbDelete(id)`@3174 via `sb-del`@3191 → `uiConfirm`@3176.
  5. Workouts: `renderWorkouts()` @3723 · add: initGym @3707 · delete: `removeWorkout(id)`@3717 via `data-del`@3713 (inside `uiConfirm`).
  6. Reminders: `renderReminders()` @8215 · add: remAdd @8275 · delete: INLINE @8231 (NO uiConfirm — direct filter).
  - ENTER: module-level `var justAdded = new Set()`. In EACH add-site, after the push/unshift, `justAdded.add(newId)` BEFORE the render call. After each render's `innerHTML=`, the render adds `data-id="'+id+'"` to the ROW element, then: `host.querySelectorAll('[data-id]').forEach(function(r){ if(justAdded.has(r.getAttribute('data-id'))) r.classList.add('row-enter'); }); justAdded.clear();` — consuming the set each render = no re-fire on unrelated re-renders (same discipline that fixed item E). CSS: `@media(no-preference){.row-enter{animation:rowBorn 520ms var(--ease-out)}}` + `@keyframes rowBorn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px);background:rgba(124,255,178,.14)}to{opacity:1;transform:none;background:transparent}}`.
  - LEAVE: shared `function animateRemove(el,done){ if(!el || reducedMotion){ done(); return;} el.classList.add('row-leave'); var fired=false; function go(){ if(fired) return; fired=true; done(); } el.addEventListener('animationend',go,{once:true}); setTimeout(go,260); }` CSS: `.row-leave{animation:rowDie 200ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,1,1) forwards;pointer-events:none}` + `@keyframes rowDie{to{opacity:0;transform:translateX(14px)}}`. (Refined off my earlier max-height idea — you can't keyframe from `height:auto`; the wholesale rebuild inside `done()` collapses the list instantly, so a fade+slide-out is enough and measurement-free. Optional deluxe: JS-measure `offsetHeight`→inline max-height→transition to 0 if you want the list to slide closed smoothly.)
  - DELETE WIRING: capture the row el at click time (closure), then wrap the EXISTING mutation in the confirm callback: `uiConfirm({…}, function(){ animateRemove(rowEl, function(){ /* the original removeItem/del/removePR/sbDelete/removeWorkout body */ }); })`. Reminders (no confirm) wraps directly. **You add a class + DEFER the existing mutation — you do NOT touch any figure. @Akashi: glance the deferred-delete path on the poison-carrying handlers (removeItem/removePR/sbDelete read owner figures) — the mutation body is byte-unchanged, only its timing moves.**
  - Reduced-motion: `animateRemove` calls `done()` immediately; `.row-enter` is gated by `@media(no-preference)` → instant add/remove. i18n: none.

  **B2 SAVINGS RING — build-ready.** Markup after `#streakPill` (index.html:1201), add `align-items:center` to `.pillbar` (197): `<svg id="saveRing" viewBox="0 0 36 36" width="30" height="30" style="display:none;flex:0 0 auto" role="img"><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="15.5" fill="none" stroke="var(--line)" stroke-width="3"/><circle id="saveRingArc" cx="18" cy="18" r="15.5" fill="none" stroke="var(--cyan)" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" transform="rotate(-90 18 18)" stroke-dasharray="97.4" stroke-dashoffset="97.4"/></svg>`. CSS: `#saveRingArc{transition:stroke-dashoffset 700ms var(--ease-out),stroke .3s ease}` `@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#saveRingArc{transition:none}}`. 30px (subordinate to the 60px hero — NO text-shadow so it never out-glows the hero, which must keep the light).
  - PROGRESS SOURCE — decisive + honest: **funded ratio = Σ(balance) / Σ(target) across boxes with `target>0`** (if none have a target → hide the ring; nothing to show). COLOR: lime if ratio≥1 (goal reached); **amber if the plan is STALLED** (any targeted box has `yearsToTarget(b)===Infinity` → at current top-up you never reach it — a REAL "behind" signal from the data); else cyan (on track). **CORRECTION to my roadmap's "on-pace lime / behind amber":** there is NO deadline field in the box model, so schedule-"behind" isn't computable — I'm swapping it for the stall test above, which IS derivable and honest. Draw-on: `requestAnimationFrame(function(){ arc.style.strokeDashoffset=(97.39*(1-p)).toFixed(1); })`. Call `renderSaveRing()` at the end of `renderSavingsBoxes()` @3126 + once on boot.
  - **@Akashi:** ring reads `b.balance`/`b.target` RAW (not ×token) and DISPLAYS only a `%`, never a kr figure → no money-figure leak, ratio stays honest under tamper. Confirm it needn't be poisoned (I believe clean). **@Mikoto:** ONE tiny string for the aria-label, e.g. `"{n}% funded"` — the ring visual is language-free. Optional nice-to-have: tap ring → `document.querySelector('[data-p="rule"]').click()` (+`role=button;cursor:pointer`).

  **B3 BUTTON GLINT — CUT (honest).** Cursor-following glint on `.btn/.tab` is NOT additive here: (1) travel distance on ~40px controls is too short for a cursor-tracked sheen to read — the wow doesn't survive the control size; (2) buttons already carry FOUR interaction layers (hover-lift + brightness + A1 tap-ripple + focus ring) — a fifth cursor-tracked layer is noise on a serious finance tool; (3) the cursor-reactive premium language already lives on CARDS (3a sheen) where the surface is big enough to earn it — that's its right home; (4) per-hover `will-change` churn on every button for a near-imperceptible payoff. If Osefe wants more button life on desktop, the cheaper honest move is a ONE-SHOT hover-sweep (a 400ms gradient sweep that fires once on hover-enter, no rAF/no cursor math) — I'll spec it on request, but my default is CUT.

  **B5 OVERSCROLL GLOW — CUT (honest).** Touch-driven edge-glow is the worst effort:wow:safety ratio in the wave: (1) iOS rubber-band + Android stretch/glow ALREADY give a native overscroll cue — a custom one is redundant and can fight the native effect; (2) it needs JS on the touchmove hot path (boundary math + paint) for a purely DECORATIVE glow that confirms no action — exactly what the hard-rails say to avoid, and the one item with real jank risk on a mid-phone; (3) unlike ripple/count-up (which confirm actions / show data), it carries a perf cost for zero information. Cheap honest replacement: `overscroll-behavior-y:contain` on the scroll container (lets the native rubber-band do the tactile job, zero JS) + optionally a static faint cyan top/bottom edge-fade (pure CSS ::before/::after, always-on, no touch tracking) for the "edge of the data" hint.
